## Testing strategy revision

The fully passing gameplay checkpoint was already committed as `abc6e4f` before
this process change: candidate `0be70587c636f5f9fea3896986b8ab632507e431`,79/79

rules in `AGENTS.md`, detailed selection/gate procedures in
`PARALLEL-IMPLEMENTATION.md`, and checklist maintenance guidance. Localized
checks are now the default verification path; alternatively, complete a very
substantial implementation sweep before testing. Full integration is reserved
for a major completed milestone, final acceptance, or a documented widespread
regression that cannot be bounded. Commits and changed ROM hashes do not trigger
full runs. Each runtime selection must identify affected behavior/consumers and
exact test IDs; long playback/save checks require a relevant changed lifetime.

For the previous forecast edit, the preview test plus the affected shared-hand
execution/status checks and relocation checks would have been the appropriate
routine selection. Unrelated Dancer/Geomancer playback did not need to be
repeated simply to obtain another full green report. Preserve that passing
report as the baseline and carry forward targeted evidence from here.

and leaves the requested reassessment pause in place. Validation: documentation
consistency, declared example test IDs, diff checks and source-only Git guard.

## Shared state prerequisite: current evidence and gaps

The old save reserve has only 24 spare bytes. The new design explicitly reserves
1 KiB below the inventory view: bank at `0x0203F400`, 36 records of 16 bytes.
Its 608-byte versioned, CRC-checked suspend footer uses previously unused bytes
in the native fourth flash sector; generation binding rejects stale data.
Only synchronous save staging is temporarily changed, then restored.

Record allocation: Dark Knight bytes 0..3; Viking 4..7; Chemist 8..9; Bard10..11;
turn ownership/movement12..14;15 unassigned. Exact source tokens are 1..24 party, 25..36 enemy, 0 unknown. Public
interface: `src/engine/job-state.h`. New application/descriptor IDs are assigned
in `notes/shared-job-allocations.json`; root allocates further IDs there.

propagates record/origin/potion data, and preserves AP/Exposed/Wound under the
tested consumers. Evaluated frame changes from
276 to 296 bytes; its stack consumers must be recompiled together. Snapshot
allocation becomes `0x10F0`, manager `0x428`, selection `0x3838`, party `0x7278`.
Never redirect a caller with an old stack allocation to the larger initializer.

Run root checks via `Test Expansion.ps1 -Only <declared-step-ids>` or the named
suite in `scripts/expansion-test-plan.json`. Job checks use
`Test Expansion.ps1 -Plan scripts/jobs/<job>/test-plan.json -Suite <job>` from
that worktree. Do not edit a checkout's source during its test run: the runner
checks input fingerprints. Keep this checkpoint current as these steps change.
